{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"rusts","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The rust on my bicycle chain made cycling to work very dangerous.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The deteriorated state of iron or steel as a result of moisture and oxidation; it consists mostly of iron(III) oxide (ferric oxide) and iron(II) oxide (ferrous oxide)."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"copper rust","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A similar substance based on another metal."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A reddish-brown color."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A disease of plants caused by a reddish-brown fungus (Pucciniales)."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Damage caused to stamps and album pages by a fungal infection."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["hobbies","lifestyle","philately"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"rŭst"},{"ipa":"/ɹʌst/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation","US"]},{"ipa":"/ɹʊst/","tags":["Northern-England"]},{"ipa":"/ɾʌst/","tags":["Scotland","Wales"]}],"upstream_line":149928,"upstream_offset":678516439,"upstream_record_sha256":"1c1c963b998c01851234e91bac75fb42d129411e48f924fdbd0921230e4a1850","word":"rust"}
{"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"rusts","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"rusting","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"rusted","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"rusted","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The patio furniture had rusted in the wind-driven spray.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To oxidize, especially of iron or steel."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The wind-driven spray had thoroughly rusted the patio furniture.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To cause to oxidize."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To be affected with the parasitic fungus called rust."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"My knowledge of Spanish has rusted over the years.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To (cause to) degenerate in idleness; to make or become dull or impaired by inaction."],"tags":["ambitransitive","figuratively"]},{"examples":[{"text":"It's very common for black cats to rust during the summer.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Of a black cat or its fur, to turn rust-coloured following long periods of exposure to sunlight."],"tags":["intransitive"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"rŭst"},{"ipa":"/ɹʌst/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation","US"]},{"ipa":"/ɹʊst/","tags":["Northern-England"]},{"ipa":"/ɾʌst/","tags":["Scotland","Wales"]}],"synonyms":[{"word":"oxidise"},{"word":"oxidize"},{"word":"corrode"}],"upstream_line":149929,"upstream_offset":678557679,"upstream_record_sha256":"2e5723f3a5262ebfb4871055b74ca1cdd135a1b6ff224049b30cabd3527f32d0","word":"rust"}
